OFFICE OF THE DEPUTY COMMISSIONER : DELHI 

NOTICE 


Any person who has any objection to the appointment of 
the said Shri Anand Sarup as Notary may file his objection 
in my office within fourteen days from the date of publication 
of this notice . 


No. 9 / 8 /64 - R S : - Under rule 6 of the Notaries Rules , 1956 
it is hereby notined that Shri Anand Sarup , Advocate , resident 
of D .4 Kirtinagar , New Delhi- 15 has applied for appointment 
as Notary . 


MANOHAR KESHAV . 


A .D .M . (I) for Deputy Commissioner , Delhi.
